$source2.WaitForExit()

exit $cli.ExitCode

if(-not $target -or $target.Name -ne 'media_transcode_realtime_video_cli.exe' -or $target.CommandLine -notlike '*composition-r259*'){throw 'CLI identity mismatch'}
Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public static class R259Console {
[DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError=true)] public static extern bool FreeConsole();
[DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError=true)] public static extern bool AttachConsole(uint id);
[DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError=true)] public static extern bool SetConsoleCtrlHandler(IntPtr handler,bool add);
[DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError=true)] public static extern uint GetConsoleProcessList(uint[] ids,uint count);
[DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError=true)] public static extern bool GenerateConsoleCtrlEvent(uint kind,uint group);
}
'@
[R259Console]::FreeConsole() | Out-Null
if(-not [R259Console]::AttachConsole(18472)){throw 'AttachConsole failed'}
try {
[R259Console]::SetConsoleCtrlHandler([IntPtr]::Zero,$true) | Out-Null
$ids=[uint32[]]::new(16)
$count=[R259Console]::GetConsoleProcessList($ids,16)
if($count -ne 2 -or $ids[0..1] -notcontains 18472 -or $ids[0..1] -notcontains $PID){throw 'Unexpected shared console process'}
if(-not [R259Console]::GenerateConsoleCtrlEvent(0,0)){throw 'CtrlC failed'}
Write-Output 'CLI caller-stop requested'
} finally {[R259Console]::FreeConsole() | Out-Null}

## 首格黑屏调查与下一轮观测

独立源码审查确认CanonicalInterval候选路径存在可达风险：consumeInputs因容量/本轮读取上限停止后，buildVideo才移除过期interval，清空即保留黑格；尚未证明当时channel存在覆盖target的帧。SourceDisplayPoint选择规则不同，不可混用。r259没有逐tick证据，不能把此路径认定为本轮根因。

参考GStreamer VideoAggregator的当前buffer选择与显式重复策略（https://gstreamer.freedesktop.org/documentation/video/gstvideoaggregator.html；https://github.com/GStreamer/gst-plugins-base/blob/master/gst-libs/gst/video/gstvideoaggregator.c），本项目不能用延长过期帧或增大容量掩盖真实gap。下一步保留策略，仅固定大小记录每源CanonicalInterval在已有真实commit后首次选黑attempt：cursor、master target、steady、epoch generation、候选前后数量/容量、过期移除数、channel深度及最后检查区间。记录属于尝试，不是发布或截图证明；channel深度并发变化且可能含控制消息，非可覆盖帧证明；不覆盖epoch禁止和display路径。无媒体引用、无新增队列，生命周期summary集中输出，SourceStorage sizeof沿既有planner固定账目计费。
首次双审FAIL：clear未重置首次miss，已补firstIntervalMiss.reset。修复后A/B2复审均明确PASS；代码冻结，Release全量构建会话88829进行中。此通过仅覆盖观测实现。
